# UI inspiration — every saved post

All 1848 saves from X bookmarks + Threads, grouped by what the design is.
Captions are short because most of these are images or video — open the link to see it.
The curated finance groups come first; everything after is bucketed by keyword, so
expect some drift. For the image-only ones, open `contact-sheet.html` and scan by eye.
